Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a replication algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 812. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 286/1.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to replication structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 812/286 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on replication structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 523. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 205 with relations in degree 1.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the structural decomposition condition on replication structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 523. The convergence follows from the structural decomposition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 205. QED.
